# Concept prompts for the whole Aethos dex

Generated by `tools/dex/concept_prompts.py` from `design/dex/species/`. Don't edit by hand; rerun the script after a species changes.

These prompts make concepts the converter can use without losing detail (`design/STYLE.md` §3.2).

1. Attach a reference sheet from `design/art/img/style/` (the starters) to every request, so the style stays consistent.
2. Generate the **front** and the **back** for each species. Pick the best take of each.
3. Save each image as a PNG with the file name given (e.g. `004_kynder_front.png`). Keep it at the 512×512 size it was generated at, without cropping or resizing.
4. Put them all in one folder and hand the folder back. Partial batches by evolution line are fine.

**Checklist for each image before keeping it:**
- The background is flat magenta all the way round.
- The pixels are hard squares.
- The creature isn't cut off.
- It faces left on the front and away on the back.
- The eyes read clearly.

## #001 Nebrel (Grass, 43×48 px)

**Front** → `001_nebrel_front.png`

```text
Gen 3 (Game Boy Advance) Pokémon battle sprite. True pixel art on a 64×64 pixel grid, rendered at 512×512 so every pixel is an exact 8×8 square: no anti-aliasing, no blur, no gradients, no half-pixels. At most 15 colors. Solid flat magenta background (#F800F8) everywhere outside the creature: no ground, no shadow, no text, no frame. 1-pixel dark selective outline, light from the top left, hue-shifted cluster shading, no noise. Expressive face: eyes at least 2×2 pixels with a 1-pixel white shine. Match the style of the attached Ichor starter reference sheet. The creature fills about 43×48 pixels of the 64×64 grid, centered, its lowest point 2 pixels above the bottom edge. Three-quarter view facing left. Creature: Nebrel, the Laurel Fawn Pokémon (Grass). A slender fawn in three-quarter view facing left, mid-step on thin legs, head up and alert. Two oversized laurel-leaf ears and a stubby sapling tail with two leaves. Limestone-tan coat with a pale belly and white spots in a laurel-leaf pattern along the back. Big dark eyes with a sun-gold glint. Limestone body, olive leaves with a silver-leaf highlight, black-figure ink outline. Personality: The fawn of Ovid's simile, all legs and nerves, with two laurel leaves for ears. The line is mostly female, as its nymph should be.
```

**Back** → `001_nebrel_back.png`

```text
Gen 3 (Game Boy Advance) Pokémon battle sprite. True pixel art on a 64×64 pixel grid, rendered at 512×512 so every pixel is an exact 8×8 square: no anti-aliasing, no blur, no gradients, no half-pixels. At most 15 colors. Solid flat magenta background (#F800F8) everywhere outside the creature: no ground, no shadow, no text, no frame. 1-pixel dark selective outline, light from the top left, hue-shifted cluster shading, no noise. Expressive face: eyes at least 2×2 pixels with a 1-pixel white shine. Match the style of the attached Ichor starter reference sheet. Back sprite: the same creature seen from behind and slightly above, three-quarter rear view facing up and to the right, as the player's own Pokémon in battle. It fills the lower two-thirds of the 64×64 grid and may touch the bottom edge. Same colors and outline as its front sprite. Creature: Nebrel, the Laurel Fawn Pokémon (Grass). Its signature features stay visible from behind.
```

*Shiny (for reference, not generated): The leaves turn autumn sun-gold and the coat pales to marble.*

One tangled mane blaze runs from the necks down its spine, and an S-curved bronze serpent tail curls up behind. A bronze collar with a black-figure meander band and pale ghost-blue eyes. Personality: All three heads are awake at last. Its mane burns with the underworld's river of fire, its tail is a serpent, and nothing it lets through the gate comes back out.

Its face sits on the second stone from the top, and a small pebble perches on the summit like a hat. Marble and limestone stones of slightly different grays, one terracotta stone, olive lichen spots, black-figure ink outline. Her hair is gathered in carved buns, her robe falls in carved folds under a meander band, and gold crack-light runs over her face, arms and robe, her signature. Glyphlet's single carved eye survives as a mark on her brow. Marble whites and grays, sun-gold light in the cracks and eyes, Aegean-blue meander bands, one Fairy-pink accent on the lips and cheeks, black-figure ink outline. Personality: The statue at the moment it wakes, with warm light showing through the cracks wherever love has softened the stone. Ovid's statue was ivory; Aethos carves it in marble, to match the Hierophant's title.

Laurel smoke curls up from the bowl into a veiled, face-like shape with two glowing eyes, and a tongue of flame hangs from its mouth like a word half spoken. A laurel wreath is tied around the bowl's rim, and a crack of light glows on the ground beneath it, as if it stands over a chasm. Sun-gold and terracotta bronze, marble-white smoke, olive laurel, sun-gold flame, black-figure ink outline. Personality: The brazier grown into the oracle seat itself: a tall tripod whose bowl breathes laurel smoke and whose flames shape themselves into words. Like the Pythia's answers, its words can always be read two ways.
```

A bronze Corinthian helm has grown into its skull with a violet eye in its slot, and its gaping maw opens on a starry violet void ringed with fangs like broken marble columns. The Harpe, a great bronze sickle with a violet edge, rises from its tail and arches over its back, and the stopped hourglass glows between its chest plates, sending violet time-lines along the seams. Slate grays, sun-gold bronze on the helm, bands and blade, a psychic-violet glow, black-figure ink outline. Personality: Time as a Titan: patient, enormous, and certain to win. It is the Devourer: a Corinthian helm like the Titanborn's has grown into its skull, its maw opens on starry void, a stopped hourglass glows in its chest, and the Harpe the whole story has been reassembling arches over its back. The world goes gray under its shadow, which is the Frozen Hour.
```

It wears the brothers' Corinthian helm and muscled cuirass in sea-green verdigris, with a fin-shaped crest of water sweeping back from the helm and a glowing trident in the visor's T. One arm is raised high and its hand becomes a trident of water the other dissolves into the surf. Cypress and sea-green verdigris, a bronze meander belt, Aegean blues for the wave with marble-white foam, a sea-foam aqua glow, black-figure ink outline. Personality: The bronze from the wreck, become the sea. It wears the Corinthian helm and cuirass the Cyclopes forged for all three brothers, green with verdigris, and below them it is a breaking wave, its raised hand a trident of water. Like its brothers it has only its lot's emblem, a glowing trident, for a face.
